       MPI_Finalize();\n        return 1;\n    }\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Number of nodes: %zu\\n\", MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        }\n    }\n    initializePathMatrix(localPath, localRows, numNodes);\n\n    // Determine the subset of local GPUs assigned to this MPI rank so that\n    // multiple ranks int extra = (localRank < rem) ? 1 : 0;\n            const int priorExtra = std::min(localRank, rem);\n            deviceStart = localRank * base + priorExtra;\n            deviceEnd = deviceStart + base + extra;\n        }\n        for (int d = deviceStart; d < deviceEnd; ++d) {\n            devices.push_back(d);\n        }\n\n        if (nodeComm != MPI_COMM_NULL) {\n globalRowStart, worldSize, timeStart,\n                                   timeEnd);\n        }\n    }\n\n    const double elapsedSeconds = timeEnd - timeStart;\n    const double elapsedMilliseconds = // Print results for external validation (integer hash-based)\n        if (printResults) {\n            print_results_int(fullDist, \"DistanceMatrix\");\n        }\n\n        // Validation\n        if PASSED\\n\");\n            } else {\n                printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n                status = 1;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    MPI_Bcast(&status, 1, MPI_INT, 0,
